# Skills · S · part 58

861 entries, skill-creator to skill-evals-broken. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **skill-creator**: Low-level scaffolding toolkit for agent skills — init_skill.py creates a new skill directory from template, package_skill.py validates and packages a skill folder into a distributable .skill file, and quick_validate.py checks frontmatter. Use when the user asks to run, fix, or understand these scripts directly, or when skill-forge delegates to these scripts during scaffolding or packaging. Don't use for end-to-end skill authoring, writing SKILL.md content, or skill design guidance — use skill-forge for that. Don't use for README writing, AGENTS.md configuration, or general agent documentation. ([wunki/amplify](https://github.com/wunki/amplify/tree/HEAD/skills/skill-creator) · ★19)
